- **deploynope-postdeploy**: &gt; Post-deployment completion check. Run this after a deployment to make sure nothing &gt; was missed. It answers one question: "Am I actually done?" &gt; &gt; This is a read-only check. It does not modify any state. &gt; &gt; **Framework Visibility:** Tag every response with **`🤓 DeployNOPE &lt;context&gt; · Post-Deploy`** while this command &gt; is active.
